Does that make sense?\n\nDoctor: Did these feelings start after a specific stressful event or major change in your life?\n\nPatient: I think so, yes. It’s been about a month now… since my daughter left for college out of state.\n\nI don’t want to make it sound like I’m blaming her or that it’s some huge catastrophe—because it’s good for her, obviously. But that’s when this really started feeling harder to shake.\n\nDoctor: For most of the day, nearly every day over this past month, have you felt depressed or down?\n\nPatient: I’m not sure I can say it’s been most of the day every day… I don’t want to overstate it. But I have been feeling pretty low a lot of the time. It kind of hangs around, if that makes sense.\n\n[Instruction]\n- Ask about exactly ONE thing. Yeah… I guess it has. I mean, I’m still getting through the basics, mostly, so I don’t want to make it sound worse than it is. But bills are piling up, and there are things around the house I keep meaning to fix or finish, and I just… don’t follow through.

And I’ve kind of stopped seeing people too. I used to have book club, or rides with a couple people I’d count on, and lately I just don’t go.

I’m not sure how to say this without it sounding alarming, but I don’t feel like I can just brush it off anymore. I started organizing my finances like… like someone else might need to understand them, and that scared me a little. Is that the kind of thing you’re asking?

Answer\n  about what you were given instead.\n- Don't deny what [Targeted Info] says. I mean, I can be doing something completely ordinary and it’s still sort of… there in the background.\n\nBut I don’t know if that’s because I’m thinking about her without realizing it, or if it’s just become the general mood lately.
